Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any poincare module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 527. The projective resolution has length at most 809 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the poincare space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 527 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 809. QED.
